# Lean file inventory

All paths below are relative to `user/workspace/uploads/`. This is a complete **machine inventory of unpacked upload sources**, not a completed semantic audit. Byte-identical copies are grouped; different hashes remain separate. Directory names do not establish chronology or active-build status.

**2,148 paths · 1,733 distinct contents · 355,063 distinct-source lines · 34,113 approximate declarations.**

52 distinct contents have curated source review; 12 have statements/comments-only triage; 1669 remain unclassified/pending. Targeted coverage is specified in the review ledger. No Lean compilation or axiom query was run.
